Credits: the KiCad boards KESE was checked against
===================================================

KESE was checked on 3,127 KiCad boards from 817 open hardware projects on GitHub. Thank you to
the authors and contributors of every one of them.

Every board belongs to its authors, and so does its license: the rights in each design, and the
terms it may be used under, are theirs alone. Their boards were used only for checking KESE and
verifying its determinism (that the same board and settings give the same files, byte for byte).
No board, nor any part of one, is included in KESE or distributed with it.

Below, for each project in the order of Corpus Testing: its repository, its license, and the
boards used, by their number in Corpus Testing, their name, their path in the repository and the
days their runs were made. The license is the one GitHub identifies in the repository (an SPDX
identifier); where GitHub names none, it is what the project's own license files or README say,
with the file it is in. "No license found" means that neither the repository's root, the folders
of its boards nor its README names one, and the boards remain under their authors' copyright. A
board added later carries the license given with it. Each repository is the authority on its own
terms. The licenses were looked up between 8 and 11 November 2026.
